# KONOSUBA Japanese Voice and Humor Ledger — Through V16

## Method caution

These are V01 distributions, not permanent labels. Forms become model evidence through recurrence, context, and contrast.

## Kazuma

### V01 patterns
- first-person narrative centered on `俺`;
- can use ordinary politeness strategically with strangers/clerks (`すいません`, thanks, requests);
- speech toward party rapidly shifts to plain/direct forms;
- internal narration freely uses devaluing reference such as `こいつ`;
- `駄女神` condenses his Aqua appraisal into a recurring insult category;
- outward speech in crisis is often short/direct while internal narration performs longer causal analysis;
- uses game/RPG metalanguage as cognitive framing, not merely decorative references.

### Humor/voice implication
Kazuma's comedic voice often comes from the gap between **long internal rationalization** and **short socially abrasive output**.

## Aqua

### V01 patterns
- initial divine/institutional presentation can collapse immediately into colloquial mockery;
- invokes `女神`, `アクア様`, and related status language when challenged;
- fear produces repeated calls, crying, pleading, and emotionally compressed syntax;
- anger/status challenge produces direct imperative and self-assertion;
- after genuine competence she often solicits praise/recognition;
- mockery is socially aggressive but does not prevent rapid help-seeking from Kazuma.

### Humor/voice implication
The key Japanese effect is frequently **status-register incongruity**: divine self-framing attached to petty, needy, or highly ordinary speech.

## Megumin

### V01 patterns
- stylized introduction/Explosion language uses grand self-mythologizing forms such as `我が名はめぐみん`;
- practical/hungry/negotiating speech can collapse to ordinary `私` and normal politeness/plain speech;
- can deliberately intensify ambiguous sexual/social wording to pressure Kazuma;
- Beldia encounter shows rapid fabrication of heroic register after being caught doing something embarrassing;
- Explosion incantation is both spell performance and identity performance.

### Humor/voice implication
Megumin's voice is **register-switching**, not permanently theatrical. The contrast between grand `我` performance and ordinary practical speech is itself comic evidence.

## Darkness

### V01 patterns
- firm, knightly/direct declaratives in ordinary and protective contexts;
- protector speech becomes concise and duty-framed (`守る`, Crusader obligation);
- sexual/masochistic excitement changes semantic content dramatically while preserving enough formal seriousness to heighten incongruity;
- post-Beldia eulogy reveals softer, sincere register;
- genuine emotional exposure produces a different embarrassment register from desired sexual humiliation.

## Translation-sensitivity entries

| Locator | Japanese anchor | Function | Class |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ch2 title | `お宝(ぱんつ)` | written “treasure” with ruby reading “panties” | L3 |
| recurring Kazuma→Aqua | `駄女神` | compact contempt label: “useless/bad goddess” | L1/L2 |
| Aqua party trick | `花鳥風月` | elevated four-character name attached to party trick | L2 |
| Megumin intro | `我が名はめぐみん` | grand role-register self-presentation | L2 |
| Megumin practical contrast | `私` in ordinary bargaining/context | register collapse from performed grandiosity | L2 |
| Beldia fight | short repeated Aqua/Kazuma retorts | timing/intensity more important than literal content | L1 |
| structural gags | frog swallowing, flood, reward→debt | causal humor transfers well | L0/L1 |

## Provisional language-level conclusion

V01 suggests that KONOSUBA's largest jokes are usually **structurally portable**, but Japanese carries substantial extra information about:
- status aggression;
- theatrical self-positioning;
- mock/real politeness;
- emotional collapse;
- dyadic familiarity;
- timing through minimal responses and register shifts.

The mature simulation model should generate psychology first, then relationship/communicative goal, then Japanese-derived register/stance—not catchphrases first.


# V02 Update

## Kazuma
- Emotional sincerity is often converted into very short abrasive output. After the resurrection reunion his spoken answer is `「チェンジ」` despite the preceding affective context.
- Internal reasoning remains much longer and more explicit than outward speech, especially around tactics, sex, and self-justification.
- His explicit trust formulation in Chapter 4 is rhetorically elevated relative to ordinary banter: `絶対に裏切ってはいけないものがある。 それは、自分を信じて秘密を話してくれた、友人達の信頼だ。`. This suggests that the narrator can switch into maxim-like moral language when a relational rule is activated.

## Aqua
- Eris's `「なっ!?この声は、アクア先輩!?随分先輩に似たプリーストだなと思っていたら、まさか本物!?」` is direct linguistic evidence that Aqua's seniority is institutionally real, even though Kazuma's labels deny her dignity.
- Serious priest register appears in Keele's rite, beginning `「神の理を捨て、自らリッチーと成ったアークウィザード、キール。水の女神アクアの名において、あなたの罪を許します。……目が覚めると、目の前にはエリスという不自然に胸のふくらんだ女神がいるでしょう。たとえ年が離れていても、それが男女の仲でなく、どんな形でも良いと...`. The syntax becomes declarative/ritual rather than whiny or status-seeking.
- Status-defense mode can rapidly become boastful and adversarial, as in `「ふふっ、そうよ！私だけこんなところに置いて行こうとしたって、そうは行かないわよ！そう、この状況ならお互いの立場は五分五分よ。……いいえ、帰り道を知り、アンデッドも倒せる私がいなかったらカズマは一人じゃ帰れない！むしろ私の方が有利な状況じゃないかしら!?そこ...`.
- When Kazuma withdraws Hide/support, the same sequence collapses into `「ちょっとカズマ！待って!?ねえ、何一人で潜伏してるの？ごめん、ごめんなさい、私が悪かったわ！悪かったから、私にも潜伏スキル使ってよ！ごめんなさい、カズマ！ねえ、カズマ様ー!!」`.
- Cemetery accountability ends in `「……………………はい。本当にごめんなさい」`, a notable contrast with her usual argumentative rhythm.

## Megumin
- Destroyer anxiety produces stammering and loss of the polished chuunibyou cadence.
- Kazuma's challenge to Explosion pride restores full declarative/theatrical voice. Voice performance is therefore confidence- and identity-state dependent.
- Haunted-house fear similarly reduces role-language and increases direct dependency language.

## Darkness
- V02 adds a vulnerable relational register distinct from masochistic performance. Her question `「……ワガママで頑固な仲間は嫌いか？」` seeks reassurance rather than stimulation.
- Bath aftermath and Destroyer epilogue show that shame/sexualization cannot be inferred from content alone; pragmatic relationship meaning determines the register.

## Translation sensitivity
Most central V02 humor remains L0-L1. High-value L2 material clusters around:
- Aqua/Eris seniority and status address;
- mock/strategic politeness;
- collapse into apology or pleading;
- Megumin's switch between theatrical and frightened speech;
- Darkness's unusually plain relational reassurance request.

No major V02 main-narrative conclusion depends on an L3 wordplay mechanism.

## Speaker updates

### Kazuma
- Formal register competence is now directly established.
- Internal prose continues to carry longer justification chains than external speech.
- Under social embarrassment, abrupt defensive speech and denial remain common.

### Aqua
- Voice ranges from mock-divine entitlement and high-status assertion to colloquial crying/blame and practical rescue persistence.
- V03 reinforces that exaggerated authority speech often appears when status is at issue.

### Megumin
- Rivalry with Yunyun shows rapid movement between theatrical competitive framing and ordinary private concern.
- Her internal epilogue self-description is noticeably sober compared with her famous stage-like declarations.

### Darkness
- Noble background requires a relational register model: inherited high-status language, chosen adventurer speech, eroticized combat speech, and private vulnerable speech are distinct states.

## Authorial paratext note

In the V03 afterword, Akatsuki explicitly notes that the series often uses unusual expressions and odd exchanges and wonders how translators handle his sometimes hard-to-parse Japanese. This supports retaining L1/L2/L3 auditing as part of the project, but the comment is not character evidence and does not justify declaring every joke language-dependent.

## V04 Japanese voice/humor update

### Kazuma
- Continues `俺`-centered narration with long internal causal chains and short blunt spoken outputs.
- Hans threat sharply reduces hero-performance language: Kazuma openly foregrounds low class/status and survival instead of preserving adventurer dignity.
- The mixed-bath/Hans sequence is high-value narrator-language evidence because later rhetoric reconstructs lust/avoidance as strategic intent; sequence-level language must outrank the flattering retrospective label.
- Low-stakes retaliation remains linguistically compressed and direct; the confession-booth interaction shows how easily he can weaponize a ritual context for personal payback.

### Aqua
- V04 greatly widens confirmed register range: goddess proclamation, Axis doctrinal authority, confession-priest role, anti-Eris rivalry, panic, crying plea, apology/defense, and follower-protection seriousness.
- **Seriousness anchor:** `ここで私があの子達を守らないでどうするの!?` and `信者の拠り所も守れないだなんて、私の存在意義がなくなっちゃう！` are direct, repetitive, duty-centered lines with much less ornamental status performance than her comic goddess boasts.
- True identity claim `私は水の女神！ アクアその人なのだから！` is pragmatically comic because the proposition is true while the audience treats it as absurd.
- `エリスの胸はパッド入り` shows sacred instruction and petty rivalry sharing the same ritual authority register.

### Megumin
- Post-death concern for Kazuma is less theatrically inflated than ordinary prank/rivalry speech.
- Much of V04's important Megumin dialogue is strategic ordinary speech rather than continuous chuuni role-language: data comparison, trip framing, Dustiness leverage, and Hans role coordination.
- The ability to enter a clean terminal-strike role without a ceremonial dispute supports the view that theatricality is selectable rather than compulsory.

### Darkness
- Noble-status discussion continues to contrast with eroticized danger talk. When ordinary peer belonging is at stake, her speech is comparatively direct and vulnerable.
- Her request that Wiz/companions not change treatment after learning Dustiness identity is a high-value relationship-conditioned voice marker.

### Interactional humor
- Axis cult recruitment relies heavily on pragmatic coercion, scripted faux emergencies, and role performance: mostly L1/L2 rather than L3.
- Fantasy-race vendors explicitly perform expected speech/appearance scripts for tourists, making register itself an object of parody.

### V04 translation-sensitivity assessment
MG01-J02 receives V04-level confirmation: central humor is overwhelmingly structurally portable or pragmatics/register-sensitive. Japanese-specific form remains important but not dominant.


# V05 Japanese Voice / Humor Update

## Kazuma
- **Register portability:** readily adopts Crimson Demon grandiose introduction style when it gains social approval, demonstrating deliberate style-switch capacity rather than fixed bluntness.
- **Narrative framing:** game/NEET experiences can be rendered in heroic-sounding Japanese without literal fabrication; social meaning is altered through selection and register.
- **Serious fear:** after the orc incident, speech loses performative confidence and becomes direct help-seeking; this fits the prior seriousness-compression pattern.
- **Final Chapter:** when Megumin says she will abandon Explosion, Kazuma drops sexual/romantic overinterpretation and responds in direct practical language, another core-cast J01 support case.

## Aqua
- **Goddess assertion:** `本物の神様` / goddess-role claims remain easy to activate in status/performance contexts even when literally true.
- **Serious care:** Kazuma's acute fear suppresses the normal teasing register; Aqua uses comparatively plain reassurance/comfort.
- **Cultural imitation:** adopts Crimson Demon introduction format quickly, showing performative register is available as a social/status tool.

## Megumin
- **Cultural grounding:** `我が名は...` is now established as a Crimson Demon social genre, not uniquely private speech pathology.
- **Identity challenge:** Explosion criticism produces sharper, more aggressive language and social leverage.
- **Private gratitude:** direct thanks to Kazuma and concern about being a burden use markedly less theatrical language.
- **Deliberate parody:** adopts an unusually generic schoolgirl-friendship style toward Yunyun specifically to embarrass her. This is intentional mimicry, not baseline voice.
- **Final identity restoration:** after Kazuma preserves Explosion, the return to `我が名はめぐみん` carries state-transition meaning: she has resumed the identity she was prepared to surrender.

## Darkness
- **Noble/name pressure:** attempting Crimson Demon theatrical self-introduction through Dustiness/Lalatina identity produces embarrassment and breakdown rather than fluent performance.
- **Protector mode:** danger-facing speech remains more direct and duty-centered than eroticized comic baseline.

## Yunyun — J01 counterexample
V05 requires a major nuance to the frozen J01 directional hypothesis.

Yunyun ordinarily avoids Crimson Demon theatrical declarations because they embarrass her. Under serious duty, she deliberately performs a full heroic introduction while serving as bait, and the act becomes the social signal through which the village recognizes/accepts her.

Therefore:

> **Seriousness does not universally reduce theatricality. It realigns register toward the identity/role the speaker treats as necessary in that moment.**

For Kazuma/Aqua/Megumin/Darkness, this often still means less theatrical, more direct speech. For Yunyun, serious communal duty temporarily makes the culturally theatrical register *more* appropriate and therefore more likely.

MG01-J01 should be adjudicated **PARTIAL / REVISE** after V05 rather than treated as cleanly confirmed.

## Translation-sensitivity notes
- Crimson Demon declaration humor is mostly L2: translation can convey the propositions, but the social fact that this is a shared cultural register matters.
- Tourist/technology jokes are mostly L0-L2.
- Kazuma's heroic framing of gaming experience is L1/L2 because pragmatic implication matters more than literal falsehood.
- V05 does not overturn the prediction that L3-only wordplay is a minority of high-value comedy.


# V06 Update

## Model-level revision
V06 confirms the V05 counterexample to the original J01 directional rule. Replace:

> serious state -> less theatrical speech

with:

> **serious state -> active role/identity becomes more authoritative -> register shifts toward the language appropriate to that role.**

The resulting speech may be plainer, more formal, or more theatrical depending on speaker and role.

## Kazuma
- Royal guest: capable of strategic politeness when useful.
- Iris private play: rapidly shifts to ordinary blunt older-brother/peer-like informality.
- Public self-presentation: lengthy, technically defensible heroic framing.
- Castle heist: thief-role theatricality **increases** after he commits to the mission; seriousness and performance are compatible.
- Private separation anxiety: internal language loses much of the public heroic frame and directly names exclusion/unease.

## Aqua
- Court: can perform correct bowing/formality when motivated, showing sociolinguistic capacity is not the limiting variable.
- Performance state: expansive confident entertainer register.
- Kazuma acute distress: comparatively gentle, practical speech.
- Domestic alcohol conflict: rapidly returns to ordinary demanding/blaming register.

## Megumin
- Royal dinner restraint: quiet/direct explanation that she is holding back because Darkness would suffer.
- Battle victory: highly theatrical Crimson Demon self-declaration is role-congruent serious performance, not a failure to become serious.
- Iris relation: competitive/older-sister role language coexists with genuine friendship.

## Darkness
- Host/noble: polished `ダスティネス・フォード・ララティーナ` formal presentation.
- Serious protector of Kazuma before Iris: calm formal corrective speech continues while physically injured.
- Ordinary party peer: abrasive familiar speech resumes immediately outside role constraints.
- `ララティーナ` remains a high-salience unwanted address precisely because it collapses private/noble identity boundaries in uncontrolled contexts.

## Iris
- Address progression is a major relational locator: formal `カズマ様` / guest relation -> `お兄様` older-brother attachment -> private epilogue `お兄ちゃん`.
- Royal public register and private age-level speech are sharply differentiated.
- Nondeferential interaction increases her ordinary competitive and mischievous speech rather than merely reducing politeness.

## Translation sensitivity
V06's most important linguistic effects are L1-L2: honorific/address systems, court hierarchy, technically truthful self-presentation, role speech, and pragmatic shifts. L3 remains important locally but is not the main carrier of characterization or comedy.

# V07 Update

## Kazuma
- `そう、俺の時代が。` is a compressed internal status-inflation marker after public reward/recognition.
- Wedding rescue attachment denial is linguistically diagnostic: `別に大好きなんて事はねーよ！ な、仲間だからっ！ 大事な仲間ってだけだからっ！` The stammer/repair works against the extreme cost of the action.
- `お帰り` to Darkness is strikingly plain. Kazuma's highest-value relational language is often less elaborate than his internal reasoning.

## Aqua
- Wedding officiation demonstrates **ritual competence used subversively**: she knows the clerical marriage formula well enough to rewrite it into an argument for taking Darkness home.
- Relationship-owned performance at the Dustiness estate remains extravagant in form but instrumental in purpose; register/performance should not automatically be equated with goal loss.
- Sacred curse removal is verbally minimal relative to its consequence: `セイクリッド・ブレイクスペル` ends a long deathbed sequence immediately.

## Megumin
- `悪い魔法使いが来ましたよ。悪い魔法使いの本能に従い、花嫁を攫いに来ました` is a strong role-aligned seriousness example. Serious rescue selects a heightened theatrical register.
- Alderp's suggestion that she will not dare use Explosion produces formal challenge-acceptance language; identity threat increases theatrical resolve.
- `我が親友` is both sincere relationship recognition and strategically selected wording because Megumin knows what it does to Yunyun.

## Darkness
- Post-return `もう一度、私を仲間にしてもらえますか……？` uses hesitant politeness that is unusual in the established party register. The shift indexes **belonging insecurity**, not desired social distance.
- `……た、ただいまっ!!` immediately follows Kazuma's `お帰り`; homecoming language resets the peer relation.
- Wedding/noble-duty scenes use controlled formal responsibility language, confirming that serious state selects the noble/protector role rather than simply muting personality.

## Chris / Eris
- Identity inference partly depends on sociolinguistic asymmetry: Chris's `アクアさん`, Eris's `先輩`, and shared no-honorific intimacy toward Darkness become evidence alongside appearance/habit.
- Kazuma's trap `そういえばエリス様...` succeeds because Chris reflexively answers from the goddess identity.

## Translation-sensitivity update
- **L0:** separation/rescue, deathbed-cure reversal, wedding disruption, community refusal.
- **L1:** rhythm of Kazuma's denial, threat timing, Yunyun recruitment.
- **L2:** noble/peer register, honorific/address identity evidence, ritual parody.
- **L3:** `バツネス` and smaller lexical/script-specific effects.

**V07 judgment:** MG02-J01 and J02 both receive strong support; major comedy remains L0-L2 dominated.

# V08 Update

## Darkness
- Private gratitude shows a marked shift from ordinary peer speech to noble/formal relational register. The line ending `心から、感謝します` is not generic politeness; it marks deliberate serious acknowledgment.
- Public contest exposure reverses direction into blunt anger/retaliation. Seriousness therefore selects role, not a universal formal/plain register.

## Chris / Eris
- V08 gives explicit metalinguistic evidence: in Chris form she asks Kazuma to stop distant `Eris-sama` treatment and return to ordinary `お頭` / `助手君` familiarity.
- Divine public form uses formal self-presentation; private Chris form uses casual thief/friend language. Both remain one person with continuous habits.
- The name Chris is tied to a flower with the language `諦めない心`, adding identity-symbol evidence.

## Megumin
- Direct romantic line `やっぱり私は、何だかんだと文句を言いながらも最後には皆を助けてくれる、そんなあなたが好きみたいです` is comparatively plain and low-theatricality.
- The same volume preserves intense hero/theatrical speech around the masked thieves and Explosion. Emotional sincerity therefore does not require abandonment of chuuni register.

## Aqua
- Repeats a `wise me learned` rhetorical frame for two different learned rules: caution after overconfidence, then discovery that church attendance yields worship.
- This parallel phrasing is diagnostically useful because it reveals intact learning capacity serving different reward models.

## Kazuma
- Advisor/merchant speech can become competent persuasion.
- With Chris, he selectively reframes profit/rivalry as town-help altruism, showing register and causal emphasis adapted to audience.
- Romantic internal narration uses dating-game `route` language as a schema for uncertainty.

## Translation sensitivity
- Most V08 humor: L0-L1 situation/causal reversal.
- Relationship/role differentiation: often L2 because honorifics, register and pragmatic implication matter.
- L3 Japanese-form-only material remains secondary rather than structurally dominant.


# V09 Update

**Evidence boundary:** Japanese V09 main narrative only. All paragraph locators resolve inside OEBPS/Text in the verified V09 EPUB. Short anchors preserve wording without reproducing speeches. “Voice” here means textual register and interaction, not an inspected audio performance.

## JV-V09-01 — The Explosion appraisal is a shared relationship register

- **Source:** part0009.xhtml P0009–0025.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Kazuma and Megumin, during their familiar post-casting evaluation and carry home.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「精進します！」 — “I'll keep improving.”
- **Form and pragmatics:** Megumin's earnest, deferential acceptance follows Kazuma's elaborate expert evaluation. Both inhabit an evaluative routine, then move immediately into ordinary complaint and teasing about the continuing transport burden.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue, with Kazuma's narrative framing; **L1–L2** for register contrast, **L0** for the underlying inconvenience.
- **Model implication:** shared stylized language can be genuine affiliation as well as performance. It does not make her practical claim about needing to be carried innocent of self-interest. **Confidence: HIGH** for the interaction; no claim that the entire technical appraisal is independently measured.

## JV-V09-02 — The same written object becomes “babysitting”

- **Source:** part0010.xhtml P0061, P0081–0083, P0116–0118.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Megumin first gives Kazuma a protective charm, then leaves Darkness to occupy him.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「カズマのお守り」 — “looking after/babysitting Kazuma,” in the second occurrence.
- **Form and pragmatics:** the earlier お守り means an amulet. At P0116, the preserved ruby explicitly reads 守 as も, selecting おもり in the babysitting expression. The written resemblance lets protective care pivot into teasingly treating the recipient as someone who needs supervision; Kazuma immediately objects to the assigned direction of care.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue; a local **L3** written reading contrast, embedded in portable relationship comedy.
- **Model implication:** affection and patronizing teasing coexist in the same domestic sequence. This is a local effect, not evidence that form-dependent wordplay dominates the volume. **Confidence: HIGH** for the ruby/form distinction; **MODERATE-HIGH** for its deliberate callback function.

## JV-V09-03 — Preference repair and reluctant deference reveal distinct states

- **Source:** part0010.xhtml P0184–0191, P0398–0410.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Darkness to Kazuma, first seeking the particular restraint she wants, later returning to ask for practical help after their quarrel.
- **Anchors/basic glosses:** 「それがいい」 / 「それでいい」 — “that is what I want” repaired into “that will do”; 「カズマ……さん……」 — a hesitant respectful address.
- **Form and pragmatics:** the immediate が→で repair attempts to disguise specific desire as mere acceptance, but the subsequent insistence exposes the preference. The later さん and polite request mark constrained dependence on the very person she has just challenged.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue; **L2** for stance and interpersonal repositioning, with **L1** repair timing.
- **Model implication:** neither “formal noble” nor “eager masochist” suffices as a fixed voice. Request, embarrassment, anger, and dependence select different outputs. **Confidence: HIGH**; the comic situation's exact intensity should not be generalized.

## JV-V09-04 — A plain vulnerable request succeeds where inducements fail

- **Source:** part0011.xhtml P0314–0350, P0353–0372, P0478–0486.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Megumin asks Kazuma to accompany her; earlier appeals use Iris, promised private time, and recognition, while the later request acknowledges her need and concern for Chomusuke.
- **Anchors/basic glosses:** 「……ダメですか？」 — “Is that too much to ask?”; 「しょうがねえなあー！」 — roughly “All right, I can't leave it at that.”
- **Form and pragmatics:** bowing, an apology for the request, and the tentative final question expose vulnerability rather than insisting that Kazuma should find the reward attractive. His outward complaint remains while his decision changes.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue plus contemporaneous internal narration; **L1–L2**.
- **Model implication:** receptive care does not require polite or heroic outward self-presentation. Do not reduce the result to a universal “ask gently” rule; their history and his recognition of her uncertainty are material conditions. **Confidence: HIGH** for this contrast.

## JV-V09-05 — A truth device tests wording, not whole relationships

- **Source:** part0011.xhtml P0451–0476.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Aqua questions a constrained Megumin, then addresses Kazuma while checking whether the lie-detecting device works.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「この魔道具は正常よ」 — “This device is working properly.”
- **Form and pragmatics:** Aqua first exploits the device to expose the inadequacy of Megumin's simple rejection of the Axis followers. She then supplies exaggerated praise of Kazuma, allows the device to reject it, and reframes the insult as a practical calibration.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue and narrated device response; **L0–L1**, with pragmatic stance relevant at **L2**.
- **Model implication:** Aqua can originate an effective social/inferential maneuver and occupy the corrective role. A rejected bundle of praise does not establish that every positive attitude within it is absent. **Confidence: HIGH** for the exchange; deliberately limited inference about individual motives.

## JV-V09-06 — Imported authority language can serve petty retaliation

- **Source:** part0012.xhtml P0512–0544.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Kazuma addresses hostile fortress knights while identifying Darkness's rank; Megumin and Aqua join after the hierarchy reverses.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「この御方をどなたと心得る！」 — “Do you know who this person is?”
- **Form and pragmatics:** an elevated authority-announcement script changes the social situation immediately. Kazuma then adopts injured, insinuating politeness to obtain accommodation, while Megumin converts the role reversal into mock-servant indignation.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue; **L2**, with **L1** staged timing.
- **Model implication:** formal competence and noble-role language are usable for grievance and bargaining, not only morally elevated duty. Darkness's embarrassment remains separate from the efficacy of the maneuver. **Confidence: HIGH**.

## JV-V09-07 — Divine self-presentation can escalate rather than dignify

- **Source:** part0013.xhtml P0256–0300.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Aqua and Wolbach in their first direct confrontation, with the party attempting to hold the enemy in place.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「謝って！」 — “Apologize!”
- **Form and pragmatics:** Aqua repeatedly substitutes status demands, classification disputes, and follower rivalry for the intended delay. Wolbach answers from an expectation of respect toward divinity, then also shifts into insults and petty threats. Aqua's self-announcement is true, yet initially fails to secure the expected recognition.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue; **L1–L2**, with **L0** for the conflicting stakes and later flood.
- **Model implication:** the active authoritative identity can produce aggressive, childish-sounding escalation. MG02-J01 does not predict calmness or tactical improvement. **Confidence: HIGH**; Wolbach's wider religious history remains her reported account here.

## JV-V09-08 — The repair captain's title rewards the desired behavior

- **Source:** part0014.xhtml P0048–0072, P0083–0110.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Aqua instructs the repair workers; Darkness and the workers address her in the role she has been assigned.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「補修隊長」 — “repair captain.”
- **Form and pragmatics:** practical sequential instruction becomes confident leadership; repeated title-address supplies recognition exactly when she undertakes useful work. She accepts Darkness as commander while enjoying her own subordinate captain identity.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue and repeated narrated action; **L2** for role/address, **L0** for incentive alignment.
- **Model implication:** status language can stabilize effort rather than displace the task. The role has no reported special powers or pay; its social meaning is enough in this setting. **Confidence: HIGH**, with portability limited to tasks in which competence and recognition actually align.

## JV-V09-09 — The additive particle makes recognition inferable

- **Source:** part0014.xhtml P0176–0189, P0368–0381, P0513–0544.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Wolbach answers Yunyun, repeatedly denies remembering Megumin, and later faces Megumin's final recognition claim.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「あなたのそれ〝も〟」 — “that of yours, too.”
- **Form and pragmatics:** the earlier P0179 question contains ordinary も when Wolbach asks whether Yunyun's name is also not a nickname. At P0537, Megumin repeats it with 〝も〟 explicitly marked. The additive item implies another name already treated in the same way, which she uses against Wolbach's repeated denial of remembered acquaintance.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue recalled and reinterpreted in dialogue; chiefly **L2** for the additive presupposition and its conflict with performed nonrecognition, with **L1** exact-phrasing sensitivity. English “your name isn't a nickname either, is it?” can retain the inference and later emphasize “either.” No specifically Japanese reconstruction is required, so the particle's importance alone does not justify L3. A narrower L1 classification is also defensible if the preserved additive inference is treated as readily portable rather than as a difficult pragmatic stance.
- **Model implication:** a consequential relationship inference depends on attending to small wording rather than accepting explicit denial alone. This supports Megumin's realization; it is not an omniscient transcript of Wolbach's intentions or proof of why she denied recognition. Kazuma's hoped-for final smile remains a separately qualified observation at P0564–0565. **Confidence: HIGH** for the wording and inference the narrative assigns Megumin; lower for any motive behind concealment.

## JV-V09-10 — Gratitude and technical mastery occupy the same final utterance sequence

- **Source:** part0014.xhtml P0534–0544; retrospective disclosed history at P0766–0780.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Megumin to Wolbach, after inferring that her former rescuer and teacher remembers her.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「ありがとう」 — “Thank you.”
- **Form and pragmatics:** she states what she has wanted to show, explains control without incantation, and quietly gives thanks before the voiced spell name. The scene combines personal acknowledgment with the specialist action it made possible; it does not switch cleanly from sincerity to an unrelated combat persona.
- **Channel/class:** direct speech and narrator-reported whispered word; **L1–L2**. The decisive technique omits the incantation; the text still voices エクスプロージョン, so “silent casting” would be inaccurate.
- **Model implication:** role commitment can remain central while its display is stripped down to a personally directed acknowledgment. Mastery enables the timing, but does not eliminate subsequent guilt. **Confidence: HIGH** for sequence; no claim that Megumin is emotionally reconciled at the moment of casting.

## JV-V09-11 — Casual language restores choice under an unfamiliar burden

- **Source:** part0014.xhtml P0412–0427, P0473–0484.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Kazuma to Megumin after she cannot explain her inhibition and Yunyun struggles with her own obligation.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「……どうしたい？」 — “What do you want to do?”
- **Form and pragmatics:** Kazuma lacks the history and initially panics at her distress. He then asks in the familiar tone of inviting her out and reuses the Crimson Demon “take the best part” formula to offer help with her own decision.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue, with explicit narration comparing its tone to an ordinary outing; **L1–L2**.
- **Model implication:** an established playful script can become usable support. This is an invitation with a stated practical boundary—he will not stop the other defenders merely to preserve Wolbach—not a promise to endorse any outcome. **Confidence: HIGH**.

## JV-V09-12 — Generic praise fails; particularized affection preserves flaws

- **Source:** part0014.xhtml P0719–0740.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Kazuma and Megumin during direct reciprocal disclosure.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「三枚目」 — the comic/unimpressive role rather than the conventionally dashing lead.
- **Form and pragmatics:** Kazuma's hesitant answer defaults to her specialty, which she identifies as a formula. Her own explanation builds through unflattering particulars—limits, variable effort, mixed conduct, poor timing—before articulating why this imperfect person is loved.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue with Kazuma's successive internal misreadings; **L1–L2**.
- **Model implication:** “likes Explosion” cannot substitute for a contextual voice grammar or a specific account of affection. Her speech does not demand that his habitual flaws be revealed as secretly admirable in every case. **Confidence: HIGH** for her avowed preference; her charitable final interpretation remains her interpretation.

## JV-V09-13 — Embarrassing self-disclosure becomes an unconventional comforting register

- **Source:** part0014.xhtml P0752–0815.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Kazuma to a tearful Megumin, then reciprocal teasing after she explains her guilt.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「恩知らず度」 — a deliberately makeshift “degree of ingratitude.”
- **Form and pragmatics:** early flustered boasts give way to a detailed account of his own failures and a comic comparison that attempts to loosen her totalizing self-blame. The self-description is socially costly rather than a heroic résumé. Megumin laughs and replies directly; this return of teasing does not mean her history was never serious.
- **Channel/class:** dialogue, autobiographical report, and immediate narration; chiefly **L1–L2**. The productive 度 formation is transparent and portable enough that this entry does not require an L3 classification.
- **Model implication:** the same capacity for framing can be used to care as well as self-exonerate. His “manufactured problem” analogy is a consoling interpretation, not proof that Wolbach engineered the original attack. **Confidence: HIGH** for the exchange and immediate change; unresolved longer-term grief.

## JV-V09-14 — A second narrator makes romantic knowledge boundaries visible

- **Source:** part0015.xhtml P0003–0016, P0029–0062, P0074.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Megumin's first-person epilogue narration and her conversation with Kazuma the following morning.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「確認できてはいません」 — “we have not confirmed it.”
- **Form and pragmatics:** inward certainty of affection coexists with explicit uncertainty about whether they have become a couple. She withholds an assumed entitlement to object, asks about his preference, and privately decides to grow her hair. Her internal knowledge of his planned outing is incomplete.
- **Channel/class:** chiefly Megumin narration, with direct dialogue; **L1–L2**.
- **Model implication:** do not attribute these observations to Kazuma or turn mutual liking into a confirmed relationship agreement. Nor should her restraint be read as absence of jealousy. **Confidence: HIGH** within the epilogue's stated perspective.

## JV-V09-15 — A letter supplies intimacy within formal obligation

- **Source:** part0016.xhtml P0036–0040.
- **Speaker/addressee and state:** Iris writes to Kazuma about his reported service and a requested escort connected to an arranged meeting.
- **Anchor/basic gloss:** 「お兄様へ」 — “to elder brother,” in respectful affectionate address.
- **Form and pragmatics:** a formal letter opening and indirect request coexist with the established brother-address relation. Kazuma's response belongs to his narration and action, not to Iris's unspoken feelings.
- **Channel/class:** quoted written communication; **L2**.
- **Model implication:** this is a small new sample of role-conditioned address. It does not supply the private deliberation required to resolve all Iris predictions. **Confidence: HIGH** for wording; limited scope.

## V09 language-model disposition

**MG02-J01: STRENGTHEN with explicit non-circular application.** Independent roles and stakes precede the observed shifts: caretaker/partner, dependent requester, noble intermediary, divine claimant, repair captain, former pupil, and familiar companion. Their registers differ; neutral or hesitant language remains part of each repertoire.

**MG02-J02: PRESERVE / STRENGTHEN at V09 level.** Major comic sequences remain L0–L2 dominated qualitatively. The charm/babysitting reading supplies a local L3 effect that requires reconstruction or annotation; the recognition particle supplies an exact-wording-sensitive L1/L2 inference that can survive ordinary additive wording in translation. The latter carries a serious relational payoff and is not a major comic sequence, so “mostly portable humor” must never become “Japanese micro-language does not matter.”


# V10 Update

**Evidence boundary:** verified V10 Japanese main narrative. Pr/C1/C2/C3/C4/C5/Ep resolve respectively to `text/part0009.html`–`text/part0015.html`; P numbers count all body paragraphs. Textual voice is analyzed, without illustrations or audio. Original ruby metadata was checked where relevant. The Gen03 model and predictions remain frozen, and the V10–V12 tranche remains open.

## JV-V10-01 — An actual personal request removes the expected teasing defense

- **Source / channel:** C1 P0175–0196, dialogue with Kazuma's narration.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** actual Megumin addresses Kazuma in the household after checking the others' likely location. She objects to the selective energy he spends on Iris.
- **Short anchors / gloss:** 「そうですよ、妬いてるんです」 (P0180), “Yes, I am jealous”; 「えっ。……あっ、はい」 (P0181), “Huh… ah, yes.”
- **Form / pragmatics:** Megumin takes up his accusatory wording and accepts it directly, then requests more attention. Kazuma's teasing stance loses its footing and contracts into a hesitant polite assent. She subsequently accepts the escort task and acknowledges care for Iris rather than making jealousy a permanent prohibition (P0194–0196).
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L2**, relational directness and answer/expectation reversal; **H1** for the request and response. The audience check and attention complaint at P0175–0176 independently establish personal stakes before P0180 is scored. His perception that this is newly unrestrained (P0183–0185) is an interpretive report, not a claim that all her later requests must use the same form. His contemporary “not yet dating” account remains at C3 P0113.

## JV-V10-02 — Ordinary expertise can inhabit a dignified teaching register

- **Source / channel:** C2 P0324–0357, dialogue and observed preparation.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Aqua teaches sheltered Iris to prepare a meal; Darkness later receives the same apparent expertise.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「完成よ」 (P0341), “Finished”; 「色んな役立つ知識を教えてあげる」 (P0357), “I'll teach you all sorts of useful things.”
- **Form / pragmatics:** confidently explanatory, benefactive teaching language magnifies a nearly instant tuna-mayonnaise dish. The preparation actually occurs at P0340, so ordinary competence is not erased by the joke. Darkness's formal apology for underestimating her (P0355–0356) supplies the status reversal.
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L0–L2**, ordinary food made impressive through audience expectations and register. Food/culture is separately flagged. Her cooking authority should not be turned into a generalized divine-service or sustained-goal success; this is one completed meal.

## JV-V10-03 — Formal naming becomes challenge, then identity-based justification

- **Source / channel:** C4 P0383–0423, dialogue during Megumin's interrogation.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** actual Megumin to a prosecutor who treats her name and daily practice as implausible; Kazuma observes.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「めぐみんと申します」 (P0384), “My name is Megumin”; 「おい、私の名について」 (P0391), “Hey—about my name…”; 「それが私の生き様だからです」 (P0419), “Because that is how I live.”
- **Form / pragmatics:** a deferential self-introduction becomes a direct challenge when the examiner glances at the truth bell. The later copular justification turns the question about one night's action into a claim about identity. Her 「前世は破壊神に違いない」 (P0406) states a belief rather than proving a former existence.
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** the continuous interrogation is primarily **L2** identity/credibility pragmatics, with a nested name-form issue that may require an explanation of めぐみん's nickname-like incongruity. The brief name joke is retained in the census. Bell silence is compatible with sincere belief (P0432); it does not validate metaphysical autobiography or make the practical question answered.

## JV-V10-04 — Divine self-legitimation follows an externally visible categorization

- **Source / channel:** C4 P0445–0470, dialogue and action.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Aqua addresses the examiner, with the party and guard present.
- **Anchor / gloss:** 「正真正銘水の女神」 (P0454), “the genuine goddess of water”; 「なんでよーっ！」 (P0459), “Why?!”
- **Form / pragmatics:** the examiner's archpriest label at P0451 precedes Aqua's rising at P0452 and expanded identity declaration. When the silent bell is called broken (P0458), her demand for recognition becomes protest. Her aura explanation later distinguishes sacred capacity from truthfulness (P0466).
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L2** status contest within an **L0** epistemic reversal. The claim of divinity is true within the established narrative, while her earlier claim to have defended the town is identified as a lie. Neither truth nor lying automatically determines whether the bell sounds.

## JV-V10-05 — “Where” versus “which” is an exact-wording-sensitive pragmatic trap

- **Source / channel:** C4 P0181, P0263–0269; C5 P0459–0464, dialogue.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Kazuma proposes a coin game to Levi, with Iris watching; later Levi reverses their roles under changed political stakes.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「どこにあるか」 (C4 P0181/P0269), “where it is”; 「どちらにあるかなんて言ってない」 (P0269), “I never said which [of the two]”; 「コインはどっちにある？」 (C5 P0464), “Which [hand] has the coin?”
- **Form / pragmatics:** displayed fists invite a binary inference that the initial question does not assert. Kazuma exploits that inferred restriction and withholds the pocket location. Levi's later colloquial どっち restores the actual two-alternative contract.
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L2**, not automatic L3. English “where” versus “which hand” can preserve both the deception and the corrective callback with ordinary wording. This is consistent with the earlier distinction between portable additive も and genuinely form-dependent お守り wordplay. The later fair wager is a serious political/relational payoff, not an additional census joke solely because of its verbal precision.

## JV-V10-06 — A name dispute is ordinary competitive appropriation

- **Source / channel:** C5 P0125, P0147–0157, dialogue and narrated result.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Megumin challenges Iris after Iris's dragon kill.
- **Anchors / gloss:** actual skill 「セイクリッド・エクスプロード」 (P0125); Megumin's 「セイクリッド・エクスプロージョン」 (P0153); Iris's 「関係ないと言いますか」 (P0155), roughly “It isn't related [to Explosion], actually.”
- **Form / pragmatics:** Megumin redescribes the technique as if it were a superior Explosion, insists on her own hypothetical success, and demands that the rival move cease. Iris corrects both the name and the plagiarism inference, finally objecting directly (P0157).
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L2**, supported by portable lexical similarity between “Explode” and “Explosion.” Katakana does not itself establish L3. Her pre-battle stammer at P0103 and the actual result at P0147 qualify her post-battle account of calmly conceding credit. This tests a particular specialist-competition mapping, not a universal sentence-ending rule.

## JV-V10-07 — Explicit protection recruits Darkness's institutional name

- **Source / channel:** C5 P0097–0109, dialogue plus observed movement/skill use.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Darkness addresses Iris and then the dragon before the party.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「私は攻撃が不得手ですので」 (P0099), “I am not good at attacking”; 「盾の一族と呼ばれたダスティネスの力」 (P0109), “the strength of the Dustiness family called the shield clan.”
- **Form / pragmatics:** she gives an unembellished capability limit while making a concrete allocation of risk, then invokes family function as she faces the threat. Her forward movement at P0098 establishes the protector role independently before the identity wording is evaluated.
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L2** institutional/protector framing, with **H1** positional and skill evidence. This is stronger than taking elevated words alone as seriousness. It does not imply all Darkness's institutional language is competent: the interrogation supplies the contrary comic case (C4 P0352–0378).

## JV-V10-08 — The impersonator's intimacy is not Megumin's development

- **Source / channel:** C5 P0488–0544, retrospectively identified by P0585; dialogue as heard by Kazuma.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Ragcraft impersonating Megumin to Kazuma, who mistakes the encounter for her promised personal return.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「その、ダメ……です……か……？」 (P0515), “Um… is that too much to ask?”; 「少しだけ目をつぶってはもらえませんか？」 (P0525), “Would you close your eyes just for a moment?”
- **Form / pragmatics:** hesitation, a negative-polarity permission question and an indirect request create apparent vulnerability. The eye-closing request has a concrete tactical consequence: Kazuma loses consciousness (P0534). His expectation is independently generated by his recollection at P0505–0509; the utterance is a tailored deception.
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L2**, intimacy cues reproduced by an impostor. It is evidence of how Kazuma recognizes or over-recognizes a script, and of the impersonator's performance. It is **not** actual Megumin's voice, desire, consent or completion of the earlier promise. Her actual direct jealousy is separately anchored in C1.

## JV-V10-09 — Failed impersonation depends on relationship-specific expectations

- **Source / channel:** C5 P0546–0579; Ep P0024–0027, dialogue heard by the captive Kazuma.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Ragcraft as Kazuma addresses Darkness, who challenges his identity; the real Kazuma listens.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「身分を捨てて」 (C5 P0550), “throw away my status”; 「平民のお前に一体何の身分が」 (P0552), “What status does a commoner like you have [to abandon]?”; 「貴様、本物のカズマではないな！」 (P0562), “You are not the real Kazuma!”
- **Form / pragmatics:** noble-sacrifice romance misfits the commoner's position. Darkness then identifies his anomalous composure and non-ogling gaze, rather than appearance or an isolated catchphrase. She subsequently prescribes a cruel vocal manner to the recognized fake (P0572), complicating any claim that recognition itself guarantees resistance.
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L0–L2** relational authentication and script mismatch. The epilogue explicitly corrects Kazuma's claim that only Iris detected him. Do not infer the undisclosed exact words used on Iris from the aftermath at C5 P0707.

## JV-V10-10 — Care, ordinary reassurance and trope-aware self-interest coexist

- **Source / channel:** C5 P0216–0239, dialogue and Kazuma's internal concession.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Kazuma reassures discouraged Iris after Megumin asks him to help; Aqua comments to Megumin in their presence.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「後はお兄ちゃんに任せとけ」 (P0234), “Leave the rest to big brother”; 「ナデポとニコポ」 (P0238), the patting/smiling-to-make-her-fall-for-you trope labels.
- **Form / pragmatics:** ordinary familiar address and an offer follow direct recognition of her effort, rather than a heroic monologue. Aqua's compressed labels expose an additional self-serving component, and Kazuma admits he partly aimed at it (P0239).
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** reassurance **L2**; census episode **mixed L2/L3** because the labels need lexical recreation but are immediately glossed in the source itself. Their mere presence does not make the whole care action nonliteral or negate its help. Conversely, help does not certify a pure motive.

## JV-V10-11 — False heroic memory contains a genuinely local lexical burden

- **Source / channel:** C2 P0482–0494, Iris's speech and Kazuma's internal response.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Iris recalls his alleged adventures with admiration; Kazuma knows the stories were misleading.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「サタンクロス」 (P0482), “Satan Cross,” tied to an anti-single Christmas story; 「ネトゲハイ神」 (P0485), a written online-gaming “Hai-god” title.
- **Form / pragmatics:** familiar cultural/lexical material is recast as heroic demon combat and a divine title. The second label uses 神 in the gaming-addiction/NEET context that his response supplies (P0486–0488). The JSON contains **no ruby prescribing a reading for ネトゲハイ神**; do not manufacture one.
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** primary **L3** for preserving both particular label jokes, with a portable **L2** frame of sincere admiration for invented prestige. The translation needs reconstruction or explanation of the lexical reworking even though the remorse scene remains understandable without it. No outside game, franchise or folklore expansion is needed.

## JV-V10-12 — Relational address and a polite refusal carry serious meaning

- **Source / channel:** C5 P0749–0758; Ep P0034–0060, dialogue.
- **Speaker / addressee / relationship:** Iris first addresses Levi after he tries to revive the marriage expectation; later she thanks Kazuma for the ring.
- **Anchors / gloss:** 「ずっとお友達でいましょうね」 (C5 P0757), “Let's remain friends always”; 「お兄様！　あ、いえ」 (Ep P0055), “Big brother! Ah, no…”; 「ありがとう、お兄ちゃん」 (P0058), “Thank you, big brother,” now in a more familiar form.
- **Form / pragmatics:** enduring inter-state friendship is extended into a courteous personal non-marriage answer. The later visible repair from お兄様 to お兄ちゃん narrows distance in private gratitude; the language change is intentional in the scene.
- **Class / implication / qualifier:** **L2**, register/pragmatic relational work. The first supplies a comic thwarted expectation; the final thanks is a serious affectionate tail. Neither address proves a legal sibling relation, a formal romantic union or a completed couple label. The cheap ring's emotional value does not follow its price (Pr P0004–0015; Ep P0036–0045).

## JV-V10-13 — Ruby and phonetic effects require scene-specific treatment

C1 P0259/P0264/P0271 spell out impaired speech as the poison affects articulation. **L1** functional slurring can be recreated through ordinary target-language speech distortion; exact Japanese mispronunciations need not be treated as a hidden clue. C3 P0104 writes お守り with ruby 守→も, establishing “babysitting”; this V10 episode supplies no paired “protective charm” reading. Importing the V09 two-sense joke merely because the written form recurs would manufacture a mechanism. C4 P0608 embeds 「スタイリッシュ・ソード」 in Iris's solemn full name: retain the brief tonal incongruity, but the ordinary English words “Stylish Sword” can preserve it. Cultural dependence, script and exact-wording importance are separate from an automatic L3 judgment.

Preserve this assignment rather than relabeling her role after the response. |

His plan may already have served her; this is a local interpersonal change, not proof of a wholly new motive. |

This overlaps the first scene and is not an independent pooled trial. |

## Language distribution boundary

The **final audited V10 census contains 112 episodes/candidates: 106 clear L0–L2, 1 clear L3, 1 mixed and 4 uncertain**, with conservative local L0–L2 bounds **94.64%–99.11%**. The original 105-episode boundary-before-classification snapshot (SHA-256 `fe23a22171efd1b6c7953292a41d38b670bf8941f8eba5f5fe0982c435e0c429`) and its 101/1/1/2 distribution remain historical. A separate full audit of 96 uncovered ranges identified seven supplementary candidates; their boundaries were sealed before supplementary classification (SHA-256 `b848131ec2757e04b18d4189e9363b616974a5786343cb93d03fe29d5186c526`). Five add clear L0–L2 cases and two remain eligibility-uncertain. No original L class changed. Source-description corrections and all added IDs are recorded explicitly.

The complete source-order registry, cultural flags, ordinary-equivalent tests, original/final counts and near-miss decisions are in the canonical `../02 Sequential Readings/KONOSUBA_V10_DEEP_READING.md`, **Appendix A**; the fixed J01 comparisons are in **§8.4**. Short lexical/name jokes remain represented, and all four uncertain cases remain in the denominator. **MG03-J02 stays PARTIAL / TRANCHE_OPEN** until the complete V10–V12 pool is read and coded. Neither a local majority nor the coverage correction creates a new freeze or model generation.

## Source and prospective boundary

## Mapping-level counterevidence

Aqua's visible repair of Kazuma's rib supplies actual service, but the fixed C2 P0487–0493 → P0498–0507 window does not demonstrate the predicted increase in practical directives: apology, spell and diagnostic question occur. This is a limited adverse language case, not failed healing. The Kazuma wallet-care window supplies internal repayment intention but insufficient comparable speech; the child-scarcity window lacks prior Kazuma dialogue. Darkness's belonging window is decided PARTIAL/source-mediated and circularity-limited: C4 P0510 reports attacks on everyone and P0512 describes unsteady/shaken behavior, while the exact insult is known through P0511's semantic report. That semantic content is distinct from the scored reassurance-seeking question form, but no newly shown independent direct insult or close local baseline establishes a clean confirmation. None is hidden by the good Megumin specialist mapping.

## J02 form-sensitive and culturally dependent cases

The nickname blends at C1 P0276–0277 and the name distortions at C3 P0078–0085 require conservative L3 retention. 姫 at C1 P0317–0325 raises a cultural-versus-linguistic distinction: an English princess metaphor may preserve the online/literal contrast, while MMO role knowledge may need explanation. The nested ショウテン threat at F P0259 can have death/climax layering, while the entire episode's flight-cancellation collision is situationally portable. Counterreading disagreements remain visible in the complete classified register rather than being changed to improve a majority. Possible genital echo in チンチン (C4 P0267/P0444) is not certified as a separate pun without a source-marked response. The V11 お守り gift is genuine charm context: do not import V09's charm/childcare joke by word matching.

The unequal windows are not a frequency experiment and do not yield a register rate.

## Voice machinery outside a catchphrase model

Megumin's vulnerable appeal can remain forceful and polite at once: the request is direct, the eyes and grievance intense, and gradual public pace matters (C1P0268–0285). Her costume of maturity in handling Darkness is not a universal speech mode; final jealousy changes the address and complaint (C3P0550; EpP0082). Specialist identity is also not one sentence ending: ordinary technical explanation, ceremonial thanks, competitive denial and powerless pleas occur within the Explosion ritual (C2P0055–0071; EpP0013–0025).

Darkness's duty speech moves between civic rule, school explanation, child-directed instruction, authority defiance and personally embarrassed objection. 私が気にすると言っている at C3P0077 explicitly restores whose discomfort is relevant; familiar speech does not make her consent context-free. Her confession requests and self-offering differ from a fixed noble performance, while the faith-magic misunderstanding lets a sincere political speech be bathos without making it false (C3P0257–0347; C5P0370–0383).

Aqua can speak as caretaker, divine authority, offended victim, opportunist or pragmatic service provider. Her truth-telling about the visitors' prior motive is socially inconvenient to the coalition but not thereby inaccurate (C4P0243–0246). Sustained healing is action evidence; no mechanical stylistic marker is required to certify capacity. In final crisis, Kazuma's care often remains command-like, and his ordinary personal self-disclosure is clearest in the quieter relational conversation. Neither global softness nor catchphrase substitution explains the distribution.

## Humor portability and local form

The accepted130-episode register keeps both original fine codes and all44 differences; no consensus L0/L1/L2 histogram is fabricated. Conservative grouping is127 clear L0–L2 plus3MIXED(H004/H047/H072), local127/130–130/130. The naming reaction, 痴女ネス blend and cute コロリン病 sound remain unresolved in primary dependence. The nickname at EpP0025 and 大物 atP0039 remain nested effects, not erased because their episodes have portable primary mechanisms. VIP/MVP can be retained as the same acronym confusion; redundant 危険が危ない can be recreated with ordinary duplication; senpai in FP0146–0155 is a hierarchy/address reward, not a sound pun. Samurai/ninja/RPG context can be culturally demanding without Japanese-specific grammatical dependence. These distinctions follow actual source forms rather than a blanket assertion that ordinary equivalents preserve everything.

The clearest changes are purpose-sensitive rather than a universal softer or louder voice. Megumin can move from reassurance to defending spell legitimacy, and from a proud offer to competing over usefulness. Kazuma can keep blunt imperatives inside actual care. Aqua can perform a useful action while retaining reward/grievance language. Darkness can use formal cover and later personal objection under several overlapping humiliations. Weak, unchanged and opposite directions remain inside the judgment; the whole mapping remains PARTIAL.


# V14 Update

## Japanese written voice and fixed comparisons

The prose supports written stance, address, syntax and pragmatic effect. It does not establish performed volume, accent, timbre, pause length or audio delivery. Kazuma’s inner **俺** commentary differs from his strategic speech; Megumin’s **私** and usual polite grammar can carry threats as well as embarrassment; Darkness’s institutional titles coexist with ordinary private longing; Aqua’s ordinary familiar speech can become the pointed **先生** of a staged patient. These distributions are contextual observations, not personalities deduced from one pronoun.

All 20 original producer comparison windows were fixed and sealed before formal assignment. The compact examples retain their spans and weak cases. The complete two-reader table below adds all 24 other original windows with both sealed judgments, explicit addressees, material written forms and original limits. ### Bounded J01 synthesis across the unchanged mappings

The clearest local forms are specific to the mapped situation: Aqua’s mana handoff and reassurance in JV14-01/IV03, Megumin’s direct relational request under admitted jealousy in JV14-10/IV13, and Darkness’s consent/protector language after physical interposition in JV14-05. Each still carries the stated false-task, already-active-role or sparse-baseline limits. Kazuma’s ordinary food/bath offer and private invitations are real care-compatible forms (JV14-08/11; IV12/15), while one-turn comparisons and the late disclosure of purpose prevent a general new-onset result.

The adverse and weak material has equal standing in the registry. Aqua capitulates to priestly ethical criticism (IV22), and her service speech often seeks attention or a companion’s continued presence rather than supplying a separate practical work instruction (JV14-16/IV23). A hell threat after a direct divinity challenge may imply authority, but explicit acknowledgment demands are sparse (JV14-18); her autonomous resumed task pursuit is separately adverse to A02’s predicted displacement. Kazuma’s self-survival and credit speech remains visible without assigning a new companion-care role after seeing convenient forms (JV14-13; IV16–18). Megumin’s romantic dominance at impending separation remains different from independently established own-vulnerable appeal (JV14-14/IV20). The literal original dispositions differ and remain unforced; 44 overlapping windows, including 33 provisional presentation groups with cross-group overlap, supply no independent-test success rate. J01 is PARTIAL locally and the frozen tranche remains open.

# V15 Update — Japanese written stance and complete paired registry

Source: MAIN-15 admitted principal only, native EPUB SHA256 `ceae1226aa01421185231c837f542c430bd289489b73276b7086bb3c216ba005`. Root accepts this additive V15-local evidence through the V15 receiving review. Retained producer/candidate pending phrases describe their authorship boundary; publication and tranche adjudication remain separate. All prior ledger bytes, Gen04 originals and adverse histories stay exact. The V15 deep reading section13 routes every major claim family to H1–H4, shown/dialogue/report/inference access, qualitative confidence and governed claim transition. Apply those weights together with each row’s finer source/uncertainty limits; actual threat, refusal, pain and failed components are not erased by gag classification.

## V15 forms and comparison limits

Written stance changes with addressee, audience, role and access to agency. Kazuma’s prose can call Serena 「様」 while his spoken position still resists a particular cost, and the post-cure narrator can change address before he can explain why. Aqua’s polite first-person divine persona can issue practical instructions, concede limited ability, attack a rival or ask a quiet personal question. Darkness’s noble intervention, personal objection and bodily fantasy require different semantic contexts even when the grammar overlaps. Megumin’s polite forms do not weaken her competitive claim or the force of her relational request. None of this warrants an acoustic description of actors, pitch, accent, timing or loudness beyond what the written narration explicitly reports.

The fixed union retains95 original records:85 primary-four comparisons and10 auxiliary observations, with190 before/after arrays. The66 overlap presentation groups are display groupings rather than independent tests. Each original mapping, speaker/addressee description, role cue, surface exclusion, limit and exact boundary remains immutable. Shared precode factual corrections are external qualifications. The producer’s complete separate formal assignments and the all 25 provisional memo were sealed before any other-reader formal outcomes or closed predicate notes were opened. This preserves separate discovery and assignment histories, not total blindness: the lineages share a native source, collaborated on the uncoded union and received bounded factual/scope QA before coding.

| Source / speaker → addressee and context | Short exact material form and literal value | Pragmatic account and scope |
|---|---|---|
| PrP0009/P0018, Aqua → Kazuma at breakfast | 「触れてない」, did not touch; 「お塩」, salt with a polite prefix | Flat denial shifts to embarrassed accountability/request. This is household self-defense, not a newly adopted service role. |
| C1P0013–0018, Kazuma → Serena privately; Dust is a narrated comparison, not present | 「持ってないっス」 means “I do not have any” in a clipped colloquially polite form. | His clipped deference follows Serena’s rough cigarette demand and Kazuma’s intimidated self-appraisal. P17–18 contrasts his ease with Dust; it is self-regarding stance, not adopted care or a universal politeness rule. |
| C1P0468–0486, Kazuma → Aqua, Darkness and Megumin after Aqua’s vulnerability | 「頼む」, request/entrust, has specific practical objects in the assigned tasks. | Support, healing, police transport and crowd handoffs redirect action. New tactical care must be distinguished from an already active care baseline and from a mapping originally frozen as quiet care. The vulnerability question’s role-declaring content cannot also become its own successful form evidence. |

It is not automatically Aqua demanding acknowledgment of her own divinity, and it is not a catchphrase frequency test. |

Actual words, quoted interior and narration must not be pooled as one speech register. |

Serena’s incredulity cannot establish Kazuma’s theology as an exact census or make plural practice a single exclusive faith. |

Erasure motive is a narrator inference; the surviving text does not show who traveled with her or completed rescue. |

Literal glosses identify the selected fragment only; pragmatic descriptions use the actual surrounding source. A short form may have multiple functions without becoming evidence of every function. Role-declaring content is excluded from successful form evidence when it alone supplies the trigger. Exact source member and paragraph locators remain attached to the complete95-row private assignment file; final public presentation should preserve short anchors, literal/pragmatic distinction, addressees and every original limit while omitting full private paragraph arrays.

The provisional whole J01 result remains PARTIAL. Some care, duty, dignity, specialist and relational directions have bounded support; sparse speech, active-role baselines, adjacent subtypes and opposite directions remain. The initial full assignments preserve adverse Aqua self-legitimation cases and the Kazuma mapping mismatch rather than remap them to whatever form appears. The auxiliary Serena/Vanir/Wiz/Eris/Yunyun observations are valuable contrasts but are not primary-four J01 trials. They retain their original descriptive status. At the initial draft seal other-reader comparison was still closed; the complete paired comparison has now been received and is included below, with both original rationales, all limits and external factual corrections. No original row is silently recoded.

J01’s independently established dignity threat does not universally require D02’s separate “otherwise sought bounded event” and prior matching privacy-preference conjunction. The independent reader’s privacy qualifications in the original voice records remain its attributable reasoning about test strength, not newly imposed universal J01 prerequisites. Actual externally imposed false blame or intimate public disclosure can independently establish a local dignity stake, while prior personal objection and sparse speech can still limit new-onset evidence.

The original IV15-24 judgments retain a substantive direction disagreement: the producer reads Aqua’s 「私以下」 as comparative self-legitimation; the independent reader distinguishes relative disparagement from the narrower divine-legitimacy/acknowledgment function and retains an adverse-direction reading with sparse/already-active limits. Other supported/partial differences concern adequate independent role cues, already active stance and exact subtype. No label is changed to manufacture consensus. “Written” here describes the textually represented utterances and inter-world calls; it does not imply diegetic correspondence where characters are speaking.


### MAIN-15 complete paired written-voice registry — candidate

All 95 exact original comparisons are retained: 85 primary-four records and 10 auxiliary records. Original origin IDs, roles, mappings, addressees, before/after bounds, exclusions and weak limits remain fixed. The 66 mechanical same-speaker after-overlap groups are presentation aids, **not 66 or 95 independent tests**. Several rows are parallel views of the same source event, sometimes under different fixed mappings.

P and I below are the two separately sealed initial evaluations of each exact record, not the origin of the window. Both initial disposition/rationale/limits are retained. P’s SUPPORTED/ROLE_LIMITED/PARTIAL/ADVERSE/NOT_TESTED/AUXILIARY and I’s more granular labels are not silently normalized into agreement; differences in observed function, eligibility, prior role activity, speech adequacy and role/form circularity remain distinct. A local functional fit does not by itself establish a newly adopted role. An already active or sparse baseline is not repaired by replacing a window.

These are written principal-source observations. No acoustic, recorded-prosody, human-review, independent-trial, frequency-rate or all-25 forecast result is claimed. In particular, any retaliation is not automatically divine self-legitimation, one speaker’s frozen map is not transferred to another, and role-defining semantics cannot validate their own form when they are the sole role cue. The J01 specialist mapping and the separate M01 literal forecast trigger must not be conflated or silently strengthened by a universal named-rival-spell/debate requirement.

All locators use the same principal-member/all-body-p convention as the humor appendix. The short exact forms are a postcoding illustrative selection within each unchanged fixed side; no full private source window or ruby XML is reproduced. “No selected illustration” means none was chosen for that side, not necessarily no speech; the paired rationale specifies true missing-speech cases. For PA10, Vanir supplies the before form and Aqua supplies the copied after form. Other displayed forms belong to the row’s target speaker unless an explicit narrative/written layer is named in the original context. External notes govern corrected source presentation, without erasing the original wording.

The [V16 Voice Comparison Register](KONOSUBA_V16_VOICE_COMPARISON_REGISTER.md) is the single full home for every original mapping, exact before/after boundary, role cue/exclusion, speaker/addressee/audience, both complete initial rationales, sparse/adverse/uncertain judgments and external source qualifications. It retains 83 producer originals plus 102 independent originals: 153 main-four/adjacent and 32 auxiliary records, 370 windows and 65 broad overlap groups. Neither 185 nor 65 is an independent-trial denominator. The original code vocabularies remain literal; different strength/eligibility scopes are not normalized into an agreement percentage.

The table is an attributable synthesis of the independent reader’s complete paired receiving, checked against both initial histories. It preserves an important distinction: predicted-compatible wording may exist without an adequate new comparative test, and already-active roles can meaningfully intensify. They are not universally excluded. Conversely, role-setting semantic content cannot also be its own independent proof of the predicted form. No particular pronoun, name, institutional noun, generic politeness ending or compulsory companion addressee is added to the frozen functional mapping.

Four disputes show why the complete windows matter. Narrow PV16-038 has the actual protective screen atC3P0438 but an after window of self-defense, a cover invocation and small-golem complaint; P records ADVERSE and I AMBIGUOUS. Broader IV16-028 includes the actual Darkness appeal0461 and **行けっ、ゴーレム**0465, alongside panic, negotiation and the final self-reward wish. P’s categorical absence claim in this broad window is factually false, and its companion-directed restriction is too narrow as a universal literal criterion; whether those actual turns function as protective resource request/command remains an attributable interpretation, not a changed initial label. IV16-038 contains a real patient check atC4P0172 but no Kazuma quote in its exact before 152–171, preserving P SUPPORTS/I NOT_TESTED and the descriptive-fit/comparability distinction. IV16-067 preserves P PARTIAL/I ADVERSE_WITH_LIMITS over whether self-reproach to companions and ensuing technical answers function as relational address/request; absence of a name or pronoun cannot itself make the direction adverse.

Genuine contrary directions also remain. Darkness’s actual rescue is followed by threats/laughter rather than protector framing(PV16-041), and public Steal receives welcome/counterchallenge(PV16-042/IV16-034). Aqua’s chosen water help gives way to retreat and self-rescue(PV16-063/IV16-056); the intake question is compatible nuance, and later accidental purification does not repair the earlier language. These parallel views are not multiple independent adverse events. The narrower D02 privacy conjunction and K04A arousal criterion do not govern all J01 dignity/care cases. Armor state, changed danger, sparse baselines and task shifts qualify identification without deleting opposite functions.

Public source evidence separates initial anchors from new presentation. Most producer anchors are explicitly clipped prefixes/context anchors; independent short-form fields are null except the two original mediated forms in IV16-036(C4P0092/0096). The register retains those initial values and adds 138 distinct short material forms, each 1–18 characters, explicitly selected POSTCODING/POSTCOMPARISON from unchanged fixed after windows, with literal gloss, pragmatic interpretation, exact member/P and speaker/context. They are illustrative source selections, not new discoveries, alternate baselines, new initial form outcomes or proof of their own role. Seven original independent phase/function recipient precisions are carried separately so, for example, Darkness’s C4P0069 refusal addresses Kazuma after the waitress leaves; the priestess’s report of Aqua remains mediated; and the money-linked blessing changes recipient. F0370’s quoted fragment explicitly retains conditional allied-hit risk, not a predicted inevitable impact or an actual shot.

A generous literal service reading supplies a strong bounded contrary alternative in PV16-012/IV16-009: Anna’s new request36 and Aqua’s actual sitting37 independently establish a concrete adopted storytelling task. Practical warning33 is before it; complaint38 and story-offer42 are after. The prediction has no universal water, paying-client, physical-crisis or professional-priest floor. Preserve both original NT labels, the independent original’s adverse-limit acknowledgment and the observed nondirective choice. Existing spiritual care, changed task/participants and unequal four-versus-two turns limit identification; they do not erase the countercase. Forgiveness in PV16-056/IV16-050 is also genuine moral service, with weaker/sparser comparison rather than automatic exclusion. Same-content exclusion is local: commitment234 can establish Darkness’s role while later256 supplies separately assessed duty framing. Urgent self-rescue does not by itself make Megumin’s vulnerable appeal ineligible, and threatened practice control may contest general specialist interests without a received named-spell denigration subtype.

Additional source repairs remain external: Wiz’s reminderC2P29 precedes handling30–33; Amaryllis stops herself at 320 after hearing Vanir rather than being physically restrained; C1P449 credits Kazuma’s role, while Darkness’s explicit own duty is234/256. Yunyun’s repair is215 rather than spoken216/219, the actual identity offer isI2P44 rather than thought43, and the silenced pair atFP49–52 is Kazuma/Megumin. Actual binding/tree-suspension/rope-pulling of Serena is distinct from imminent threatened execution; no completed death/assault is shown. Original initial descriptions and labels stay exact.

The system-level local result remains PARTIAL: bounded general specialist directions coexist with adverse protector/dignity/service directions and unresolved tactical-care/relational-function disputes. Each mapping’s prospective repeated criterion needs at least two distinct fully eligible adequate comparisons; overlapping views cannot supply the second. One branch’s support does not erase another’s adverse case. The V16–V17 tranche is open, no formal role is remapped after outcome, and no acoustic performance is inferred from printed style or ruby.

## State and confidence transitions

PRESERVE the Gen05 functional mapping design and circularity rule; STRENGTHEN bounded general specialist evidence from two distinct episodes, with overlapping views counted once. PRESERVE the adverse protector/dignity/service directions and OPEN the disputed comparative strength/functional interpretations described above. DOWNGRADE any unqualified inference from politeness, one clipped prefix, a name/pronoun, a new institutional noun, or absence of a new onset to full fit/failure. H1 covers written utterances/actions and recipient context; H2 covers comic stance/magnitude; role/motive interpretations remain attributed and MODERATE unless independently explicit. No new universal character voice, acoustic feature, human independence or reliability percentage is established.
